### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026

The Japan Research Institute, Limited- ８ -

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，Goldman Sachs，凱基證券整理，註：右上圖之大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)企業為Amazon、Alphabet、Microsoft、Oracle
► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐
熱門議題
美股第三季獲利成長持續上修，科技為主要支撐 CSP雲端營收加速，資本支出變現跡象持續受驗證
評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張
熱門議題財報展望

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

### Page 8

內部參考文件請勿外流
► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 防禦搭配科技股，以槓鈴策略穩中求勝!

### Page 17

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 405: RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Sticking with the plan
• The RBNZ left the OCR at 2.75% - a shock to
the market.
• The committee voted 4:3 against a 25bp
hike, with Governor Breman within the
“on hold” camp.
• The RBNZ delivered on its September strategy
of pausing to assess the impact of OCR
increases done to date.
• The MPC remains of the view that the OCR will
increase eventually, although views on the
timing differ among members.
• Westpac expects a 25bp hike in December and

continues to expect an eventual peak OCR of
4% in September next year.
• However, a December hike is not a done
deal and will depend on data on fuel prices,
inflation expectations and second-round
pricing pressures and information on evolving
global economic risks that accumulate in the
next six weeks.
Official Cash Rate forecasts

2016 2018 2020 2022 2024 2026 2028

RBNZ September 2026 MPS

Westpac forecast

Sources: Bloomberg, RBNZ, Westpac
Forecasts
OCR remains at 2.75%, further hikes remain
their base case.
Today the RBNZ left the OCR unchanged at 2.75%. This
was a shock to markets and most analysts as a 25bp hike
had been nearly fully priced by markets. The decision
went to a vote which resulted in a 4:3 split in favour of an
unchanged OCR.
The RBNZ has followed through on the pause it signalled
for this meeting back in the September Monetary Policy

Statement. All MPC members continue to see a case for a
higher OCR in time, but there are different views around
the timing of those increases. The Governor noted that
further interest rate increases remain likely in coming
meetings but are conditional on the whether the outlook
evolves in line with expectations.
The key case made by more hawkish MPC members
(Silk, Gourley and Conway) put greater weight on the
persistence of underlying inflation and the risk that higher

inflation becomes entrenched in price-setting behaviour.
They noted that core inflation has been above levels
consistent with the 2% target midpoint for some time,
28 Oct 2026 | Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review.

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
and viewed aggregate economic growth as relatively
resilient despite weakness among many households and
businesses. In their view, tightening now would guard
against sustained inflation pressures and reduce the risk
of needing to do more later.
The case made by the majority in favour of no change
in the OCR (Breman, Hansen, Gai and Williams) argued
that the two recent OCR increases warranted more time
to assess their effects. They placed weight on inflation

having been weaker than expected in the Q3 data (the
Governor emphasised this point in answer to questions
at the press conference), smaller-than-anticipated
indirect effects from higher fuel prices, stable inflation
expectations and subdued wage growth. They also
emphasised downside risks to the recovery, particularly
the continued weakness in household consumption and
investment, and judged that there was little evidence
so far that businesses were using higher costs as an

opportunity to rebuild margins.
One point of note today is that the RBNZ seems
more concerned about risks to the global outlook,
notwithstanding its recent resilience – possibly
channelling some recent comments made by the IMF. The
RBNZ notes that geopolitical events, high energy prices,
and high and rising public and private debt levels pose
downside risk to global growth. It also notes uncertainty
about whether the surge in AI investment will be justified

by economic fundamentals. In addition, the MPC debated
whether higher global interest rates reflected a higher
neutral rate – due to higher inflation expectations and
demand for funding – or posed the risk of bringing
forward a potential global downturn (possibly leading to a
rapid decline in the event of an abrupt global adjustment).
The exchange rate has adjusted sharply lower today,
reflecting the market shock. The NZD/AUD exchange rate
has moved to a new cycle low of around 0.7980 at the

time of writing. This is in line with our year-end target for
the NZD/AUD exchange rate of 0.79 cents. Compounding
the downward pressure on the NZD/AUD exchange rate
has been strong CPI data in Australia combined with
hawkish RBA commentary. Our colleagues in Australia
continue to expect a Cash Rate hike in Australia on
Melbourne Cup Day and some risk of a further Cash
Rate rise in February. Hence downward pressure on the
NZD exchange rate seems a reasonable prospect given

interest rate differentials are weighing heavily on the NZD.
The RBNZ does not sound especially perturbed by the
prospect of weakness in the exchange rate and noted
that the low exchange rate is supportive of the medium-
term prospects of the export sector and the economy
more broadly.
Westpac outlook – December hike likely, but
not done deal.
We changed our forecasts from no change in the OCR
at the October meeting to a 25bp hike in the wake of the

CPI data last week. Given the absence of the hike today
we are reverting to the OCR forecast profile we had had
in place since July – i.e. one further 25bp hike in 2026
and then 25bp increases in the February and March
2027 meetings, followed by a slower pace of increases
at the June and September Monetary Policy Statement
meetings. Our forecast of a peak OCR of 4% from
September 2027 has remained unchanged throughout, as
has our assessment of a 3.75% neutral OCR.

That said, in the current uncertain environment, it goes
without saying that the evolution of monetary policy will
depend on how both global events and key data evolve
(we summarise the near-term economic diary below).
Therefore, as the RBNZ noted itself today, the exact timing
of the tightening profile is highly uncertain and even the
tightening we forecast at the December meeting should
not be regarded as a done deal.
Things to watch ahead of the 9 December

meeting.
The RBNZ’s next policy review is on 9 December, when it
will also publish a full Monetary Policy Statement (MPS)
and refreshed economic forecasts. How the RBNZ’s policy
stance evolves between now and then will depend on
both international and domestic factors.
Regarding the former, the MPC has clearly become
more concerned about risks to the global economic
outlook, stemming from geopolitical events, high energy
prices, high and rising public and private debt levels and

uncertainty about whether the surge in AI investment will
be justified by economic fundamentals. So, the RBNZ will
pay close attention to any developments that suggest
that downside risk to the global outlook is materialising.
There are only a few important domestic economic
indicators scheduled for release ahead of the 9 December
meeting (by contrast there is a full slate of indicators due
for release prior to the following meeting on 10 February).

We think the following are the key ones to watch.
• Q3 labour market surveys
(4 November): Indicators from these surveys will cast
light on economic activity in Q3, capacity pressures
in the labour market and whether the lift in headline
inflation is impacting growth in wages and salaries.
• Q4 RBNZ surveys
(17 November, 20 November, 24 November): Inflation
expectations measures from the RBNZ’s surveys of
professional forecasters, households and businesses

will be analysed to assess the impact of elevated
headline inflation.

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• October Selected Prices
(17 November): These pricing indicators will provide a
partial read on how inflation is tracking early in Q4.
• October/November ANZ Business Outlook
(29 October/30 November): The ANZ surveys will
provide a timely account of how business activity and
pricing behaviour is responding to the sharp rebound
in oil prices.
• October PMI and PSI surveys
(13 November/16 November): These may provide some

early insight regarding GDP growth in Q4.
In addition to the above, we will monitor a range of
other high-frequency indicators, such as monthly data
on filled jobs, consumer spending, building consents,
housing market activity and prices, job ads and consumer
confidence. We will also be paying close attention
to developments in prices for New Zealand’s key
export commodities.
Finally, with the General Election taking place on
7 November, it is possible that opinions regarding the

likely path of monetary policy could be impacted should
a Labour Party-led government – with an intention
to restore the RBNZ’s dual mandate – seem likely to
be formed.

## Document 456: Why hasn’t there been a major financial crisis in recent years_ (2026-11-03)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Why hasn’t there been a major financial crisis in recent years_ (2026-11-03).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Why hasn’t there been a major 
financial crisis in recent years?

1 
Authors 
Henry Allen 
Macro Strategist

Peter Sidorov, CFA 
Senior Economist

Camilla Siazon 
Research Analyst

November 3, 2026 
— The economist Hyman Minsky warned, in essence, that “stability is destabilising” . He 
argued that a lengthy period of financial stability could be problematic, as it induced 
risk-taking and complacency amongst investors, laying the foundation for the next 
crisis. For example, lenders relax their terms and borrowers are more willing to extend 
themselves, as nobody wants to miss out. This dynamic makes economies inherently 
prone to financial panics and boom-bust cycles. 
— But since the 2008 crisis, and the subsequent sovereign crisis in the Eurozone, we 
have yet to see another financial crisis happen on a scale that had a lasting market 
and macroeconomic impact. There have been clear moments of concern, as in early 
2023 with the regional bank turmoil, but nothing that has caused a lasting impact. So 
this led us to ask why. 
— In this paper, we examine why the post-GFC era has seen fewer financial crises. 
Explanations include the reforms made after 2008 to avoid a repeat, the lower 
proportion of financial assets held by the banking system, as well as the speedier 
reaction of policymakers when wobbles have occurred. We then consider a few 
candidates that could cause the next crisis, including AI vulnerabilities, private credit, 
and stretched fiscal positions. 
— Ultimately, financial crises haven’t been abolished, but the reforms of recent years 
have made it harder for shocks to propagate through the traditional banking system. 
Moreover, policymakers have become far more inclined to intervene when initial signs 
of stress have emerged.

### Page 2

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Page 2 Deutsche Bank AG
Section 1: What is a financial crisis, and what causes them?
There isn’t a single technical definition of a “financial crisis”, and they come in many
different types. Commonly, we think of banking crises like 2008, but they can also
encompass currency/balance of payments crises, debt crises and other forms too.
Given currency and sovereign crises have been more common in emerging markets
(the UK's ERM crisis in 1992 and the euro crisis being the two most notable DM

occurrences), historical analysis in developed economies has tended to focus
heavily on banking crises. Non-bank funding or market-liquidity crises are often not
categorised separately in the literature, but they have become more common in
recent years. This includes the "dash-for-cash" stress at the onset of the Covid
pandemic in March 2020 and the 2022 LDI crisis in the UK.
But for all the different types of financial crisis, there are common themes between

them. Often, they are accompanied by asset bubbles that burst in a non-linear
fashion. We generally see a severe tightening in credit conditions, which in turn
flows through into a broader economic contraction. Frequently, there are
contagion-like dynamics that can be difficult to stop. And in the modern world, they
usually require policymakers to step in, stabilising the situation with interventions.
At risk of oversimplification, we note four factors that determine the emergence and

the severity of a financial crisis. The first three are well-established in the financial
literature, sometimes pooled together into a build-up and a crisis phase
(Brunnermeier & Oehmke 2013), while the fourth has become increasingly
important over the past 15-20 years:
1.  Underlying imbalances. Bubbles and financial imbalances build during
the run-up phase, leaving accumulated risks that materialise when the
crisis erupts. This is the build-up typically described by the early phases of

Kindleberger's model of financial crises.
2.  Trigger. This is the shock that turns an existing vulnerability into clear
financial stress. These are often very difficult to predict and we mostly leave
these outside the focus of this report.
3.  Amplification and financial system fragility. Various factors determine
whether a shock propagates through the financial system, and the
intensity of that shock. These include leverage, liquidity and maturity

mismatches, interconnectedness, and the concentration of exposures.
Other channels in the financial system can then amplify the shock,
including bank runs, fire sales and margin calls, and negative net worth
effects, sovereign–bank loops etc.
4.  Intervention/policy response. This has been increasingly important in
recent decades as the size and scope of policy response to crises has
increased. The outright size can vary, and intervention can occur across

several dimensions, ranging from direct intervention in financial markets,
to counter-cyclical macroeconomic policy that mitigates the impact on the
real economy.
Clearly, these factors are not independent and the interaction between them is what
determines the severity of a financial crisis.

### Page 3

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Deutsche Bank AG Page 3
One important aspect is the joint role that underlying imbalances and amplification
play. While theoretical models such as Diamond & Dybvig highlight the potential for
a bank run that triggers a broad crisis, empirical research shows that in practice, it
is exceedingly rare for a bank run to lead to bank failure in the absence of an
underlying solvency issue (Correia, Luck and Verner 2026). In other words, the

presence of amplification channels alone does not lead to a crisis.
Conversely, in the absence of amplification, it is possible to get significant
turbulence in asset prices, but without a financial crisis. For instance, a bear market,
wider credit spreads, or even a recession do not in and of themselves imply there
is a financial crisis. This was seen clearly with the bursting of the dot com bubble,
a severe equity decline but not a financial crisis, though it did cause a recession. At

the same time, in the chart below, we can see several US equity bear markets that
took place outside of recessions. On other occasions, however, a “Minsky
Moment” can then happen, where even a seemingly minor event can trigger a high-
profile incident that cascades across the financial system.
Figure 1: S&P 500 drawdowns over time, with shaded recessions showing select episodes when the index fell over 20%
without coinciding with a recession
Source :Bloomberg Finance LP, NBER, Deutsche Bank Research

Looking back through history, we can see how variation in these factors has led to
different regimes over time in terms of frequency/severity of financial factors.

### Page 4

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Page 4 Deutsche Bank AG
Section 2. Why have financial crises been more or less likely
in certain periods of history?
1. Pre-WWII: More frequent financial instability, albeit under a very
different system.
Financial instability was a recurrent feature of the pre-WWII era. During the 19th
and early 20th centuries, there were frequent panics that often led to major slumps.
For instance, the Panic of 1873 sparked a period often known as the “Long

Depression”, which extended into the 1890s for many countries.
In part, financial panics occurred more frequently because policy operated under
very different settings. For instance, the Gold Standard as the monetary system
constrained a countercyclical policy response and transmitted deflation
internationally, while falling prices increased real debt burdens. Moreover, the
Federal Reserve wasn’t established until 1913, and the lack of an effective lender of

last resort meant there was a private intervention during the US Panic of 1907. The
first wave of globalisation also brought new vulnerabilities. Between 1870 and
1913, many emerging economies accumulated debts denominated in gold or
foreign currency, leaving them exposed when capital inflows reversed or exchange
rates weakened.
The biggest and most famous crisis occurred in 1929, when the Wall Street Crash
of that year sparked the Great Depression. The length and depth of that crisis helped

to stimulate the study of modern macroeconomics, with huge efforts to prevent a
repeat over the years ahead. Indeed, the failures of the response to the Great
Depression played a large role in the much swifter response to the 2008 financial
crisis, helping to avoid a second Great Depression.
That experience established much of the modern financial safety net that exists to
this day. For instance in the US, the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation was

established in 1933. The US and many other countries came off the Gold Standard
as well in this period. There were also regulatory reforms, and the Glass-Steagall Act
separated out commercial from investment banking.
2. 1944-1971: The Bretton Woods era coincided with fewer financial
crises
Around the end of WWII, economic policymakers sought to create an international
monetary system for the post-war era. The relative financial calm that followed

reflected a combination of restrictions on international capital movement, tightly
regulated domestic financial systems, and a system of fixed exchange rates that
aimed to provide stability and prevent the competitive devaluations that occurred
in the 1930s. This saw exchange rates pegged against the US Dollar, which in turn
was convertible to gold at $35 per ounce.
At the same time, capital controls helped preserve monetary policy flexibility, while

maintaining the system of fixed exchange rates. These controls had several
downsides but they also insulated countries from “sudden stop” crises that can
occur, when capital rapidly flees a country. Moreover, capital controls prevented
the rapid buildup of cross-border systemic leverage. So it was more difficult to
generate a global financial crisis in the same way.

### Page 5

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Deutsche Bank AG Page 5
Figure 2: Gold price (USD/oz, log scale)
10
100
1000
1920 1940 1960 1980 2000 2020
In the Bretton Woods
era, the dollar was tied
to gold
Source : Finaeon, Deutsche Bank
Alongside capital controls, there were also domestic reforms in the aftermath of the
Great Depression, which sought to avoid a repeat. As mentioned earlier, the US
Glass-Steagall Act of 1933 separated commercial banking from investment

banking. Another example of the era’s regulated financial system in the US was
Regulation Q, which imposed maximum interest rates that commercial banks
could pay on deposits. That meant depositors could not search for yield and cause
a run on deposits as easily. Another rationale was that with institutions not
competing for deposits, it would avoid a compression in the margin between the
rates banks paid on their lending and borrowing, which would disincentivise a push

into riskier investments.
Finally, economic growth was also stronger in this period, with the period between
WWII and the 1973 oil shock often described as the “Golden Age”. This played a role
in avoiding financial crises too, as faster growth helped keep debt default rates
down, avoiding the kind of “trigger” events above that might spark a financial crisis.
Coupled with financial repression, strong growth also helped to bring down the
high post-war public debt burdens.

Over time however, the peg between the dollar and gold found itself under growing
strain. The economist Robert Triffin identified the “Triffin dilemma”. Supplying the
world with dollar reserves resulted in outstanding dollar liabilities around the world
that exceeded the gold reserves in the US, weakening confidence that dollars could
ultimately be redeemed for gold. Conversely, restricting dollar supply to protect
those reserves and preserve convertibility risked depriving the system of the

liquidity needed to support trade and growth.
These tensions intensified in the late 1960s amid expansionary US policies,
including spending on the Vietnam War and Great Society programmes. The
continued drain in US gold reserves eventually led the US to suspend the
convertibility of the US Dollar into gold, effectively ending the Bretton Woods
system. Initially, there were attempts to bring some kind of fixed-exchange rate
system back, but the world of fiat currencies (with no link to a precious metal)

persists to this day.

### Page 6

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Page 6 Deutsche Bank AG
3. 1971-2008: Financial crises become more frequent after the
breakdown of Bretton Woods to the Global Financial Crisis
After the breakdown of the Bretton Woods system, financial crises became a more
regular occurrence. While the end of the gold convertibility did not by itself trigger
future crises, it opened the door for wider financial liberalisation, with the resulting
rise in capital mobility and looser domestic financial regulation then helping to

facilitate a global build-up of imbalances.
The 1980s and 1990s were a period of frequent crisis events. The transition away
from capital controls meant that capital could flow more freely across borders.
However, this could also cause a “sudden stop” if investors suddenly pulled their
capital out of a country, contributing to bank runs and currency collapses.
Moreover, many emerging markets borrowed in foreign currencies like the US
Dollar, meaning that a depreciation in their own currency would see this USD-

denominated debt surge in local currency terms. This meant it was a particular
problem if US interest rates were increasing, as it became harder for many
emerging markets to repay their debts. This was a contributing factor behind the
Latin American debt crisis in the early 1980s. And the rate hikes also had domestic
ramifications, being a contributing factor behind the Savings and Loan crisis in the
United States.
With capital flowing more freely, that also played a role in the 1994 Mexican Peso

Crisis, and the 1997 Asian Financial Crisis. A sudden reversal in capital flows led to
crisis and ultimately recession in many countries. The following year, the Russian
financial crisis then took place, and fears grew in the United States about the wider
ramifications, as the hedge fund Long-Term Capital Management (LTCM) came
under pressure. Indeed, this period saw 75bps of pre-emptive rate cuts from the
Federal Reserve.
This volatility also saw the Fed proactively seek to step in and smooth out the

economic volatility. This was broadly successful, but in the process also helped
engender complacency and a further buildup of leverage. Indeed, after the dot com
bubble burst, the federal funds rate was cut to just 1% in mid-2003, where it
remained until mid-2004.
This period saw clear optimism that another Great Depression was highly unlikely.
For instance, Nobel Prize-winning economist Robert Lucas argued in his 2003 AEA
Presidential Address that “macroeconomics in this original sense has succeeded:

Its central problem of depression prevention has been solved, for all practical
purposes, and has in fact been solved for many decades.”
However, as we now know, the 2008 financial crisis occurred, driving the biggest
global downturn since the Great Depression of the 1930s. It turned out that Minsky
had been correct, and a lengthy period without a crisis had once again helped to
induce complacency, laying the foundations for a repeat.

### Page 7

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Deutsche Bank AG Page 7
Figure 3: DM economies have avoided systemic financial
crises since the post-GFC period

Section 3: The Post-2008 world: Why have we not had
another big financial crisis?
Since the Global Financial Crisis of 2008, we have thankfully avoided a major
financial crisis. Despite clear moments of stress, most notably around the collapse
of SVB in March 2023, we have not seen a similar wave of bank collapses on that
scale. There are several components to this.
First, post-GFC deleveraging cleared out risks in the system, with countries and
sectors that had excess indebtedness before the GFC experiencing pronounced

declines in leverage.
Second, there have been reforms explicitly designed to avoid a repeat of 2008.
After the crisis happened, there was a clear recognition that gaps in regulation had
exacerbated the crisis, leading to effects that cascaded across the financial system.
For instance, the Basel III reforms required banks to hold more equity, with a
minimum requirement for Common Equity Tier 1 (CET1) capital. They also
introduced the liquidity coverage ratio, which requires banks to hold enough high-

quality liquid assets to ensure they can cover their net cash outflows under a 30-day
stressed scenario. In addition, the net stable funding ratio was brought in, requiring
banks to have sufficient stable funding that exceeded the required amount of stable
funding for a one-year stressed scenario.
At the national level, further reforms were also introduced. In the US, the Dodd-
Frank Act was introduced, with measures including the Volcker Rule that restricted

banks from engaging in proprietary trading, and required the Federal Reserve to
perform an annual stress test of large institutions. Meanwhile in the European
Union, which also experienced a sovereign debt crisis, reforms included a “Single
Supervisory Mechanism”, whereby banking supervision was brought under the
ECB, ensuring harmonised regulatory standards.

### Page 8

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Page 8 Deutsche Bank AG
Third, the share of financial assets held by banks has fallen since the 2008 crisis.
This hasn’t eliminated risk, but commercial banks are funded by retail deposits that
can quickly fly. By contrast, non-bank financial institutions (NBFIs), e.g. hedge
funds, are not as prone to these bank run dynamics. For instance, a private credit
closed-ended fund is not one where investors can simply redeem their funds on

demand.
We can see this shift in the data. Looking at the FSB’s group of 21 plus the Eurozone,
we can see that the share of assets held by banks has fallen from a peak of 46% in
2008, to 38% in 2024. By contrast, the share held by NBFIs has risen from 43% to
51%.
Figure 5: Share of Assets of Financial Corporations (FSB 21 + EA Group)
35%
40%
45%
50%
2002 2006 2010 2014 2018 2022
Banks Nonbank Financial Intermediation Sector (NBFI)
Source :Haver Analytics, Deutsche Bank

Fourth, the lessons of 2008 have meant that when the financial system has had
wobbles since, policymakers have acted with incredible speed to stop the
turmoil.
This was most obvious when the Covid-19 pandemic began. Whilst a pandemic
was very different to a financial crisis, there was initially huge turmoil in financial
markets. Indeed, the S&P 500 fell by more than a third in just over a month, a decline
at a speed and scale unseen since the Great Depression. Meanwhile, US HY credit

spreads soared to levels last seen around the financial crisis, and at the height of the
turmoil, there was a huge spike in real yields, despite the economy entering a
recession.
Against this backdrop, central banks wasted no time in acting. For instance, the Fed
delivered an emergency 50bp rate cut, followed by another 100bp rate cut.
Moreover, they swiftly resumed Quantitative Easing, and announced the
establishment of multiple new facilities to ensure the smooth functioning of credit

markets. These included a Primary Market Corporate Credit Facility, to support new
bond and loan issuance, along with a Secondary Market Corporate Credit facility,
which offered liquidity for outstanding corporate bonds. Meanwhile, a third facility,
called the Term Asset-Backed Securities Loan Facility, was set up to enable the
issuance of asset-backed securities. Similarly in Europe, the ECB set up a new
Pandemic Emergency Purchase Programme (PEPP), which ultimately had a total

envelope of €1.85tn.
Whilst the pandemic caused a huge economic shock, the swift action meant it did
not translate into a financial one, and the brief tightening in credit conditions was

### Page 9

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Deutsche Bank AG Page 9
unwound over the coming months.
A similar pattern was seen during the regional banking turmoil of March 2023. This
was a financial panic, which initially began with the collapse of Silicon Valley Bank.
However, this began to spread, and Signature Bank also failed. Then in Europe,
Credit Suisse came under investor scrutiny and saw large deposit outflows, which
culminated in a purchase by UBS that included guarantees from the Swiss

government. This coincided with huge market moves, with the 2yr Treasury yield
posting its biggest daily decline in over 40 years, with a 61bps decline on March 13.
However, policymakers swiftly moved to stop any contagion. The US Treasury,
Federal Reserve and the FDIC released a joint statement stating that all deposits at
SVB would be protected, and that the Federal Reserve would provide additional
funding to ensure banks could meet the needs of depositors. Meanwhile, the Fed’s

balance sheet briefly expanded again, despite the fact it had begun the process of
quantitative tightening. These actions ultimately meant the crisis proved contained
and did not lead to a wave of further bank collapses in the manner of 2008.
Figure 6: Federal Reserve Total Assets (USD trillions)

Fifth, policymakers have also responded to recent shocks with huge fiscal

transfers, meaning that debt has migrated from private towards sovereign
balance sheets.
Whilst global debt has risen slightly over the last decade, if you dig deeper into the
numbers, that’s entirely driven by a rise in government debt. If we compare the
statistics for Q2 2026, relative to Q2 2016, there’s actually been a decline in debt for
households, financial corporations, and nonfinancial corporations. The only one of
the four segments that’s gone up in the last decade is government debt.

In relative terms, the transformation is even bigger if we look back to 2007, before
the Global Financial Crisis kicked off. Back then, government debt was the smallest
of the four categories, but because of the GFC and the pandemic, it’s now become
the biggest of the four categories.
That in turn has led to separate issues, with government bond yields at multi-year
highs, alongside rising net interest spending. So the fiscal space has become
limited. However, public balance sheets don’t face the same kind of system-wide

run that a bank would.

### Page 10

Thematic Research

Overall, the post-2008 experience offers three key forward-looking lessons. First,
stronger bank balance sheets and the shift towards non-bank finance suggest

future stress may originate outside banks. As such, historical analyses centred on
banking crises may provide a less complete guide. Second, policymakers have
become increasingly effective at containing liquidity pressures and amplification.
And third, the shift in debt towards sovereign balance sheets has cushioned private-
sector shocks but narrowed the capacity for future intervention.
Section 4: What are potential candidates for the next crisis?

This section considers some of the current risks in the financial system and whether
they could be sowing the seeds for the next financial crisis. This section is not
designed to be a comprehensive list of financial risks, but rather an assessment of
three important areas where risks have risen in recent years.
1. Could AI spark broader vulnerabilities?
A huge driver of the current equity bull run has been the growth in AI-related stocks.
This report won’t explore the debate as to whether this is a bubble, but examine

whether a correction could eventually propagate into a systemic financial crisis. As
noted earlier, a reversal in the investment cycle need not cause a crisis in itself, and
the bursting of the dot com bubble also did not lead to a broader financial crisis.
Nevertheless, there are issues that could spark vulnerabilities. First, the rapid rise
in AI capex raises questions about whether the investment will pay off. The AI build-
out is on course to exceed the scale of past technological investment cycles, which

have often involved overinvestment even when the technologies ultimately proved
transformative. Second, funding is shifting from predominantly operating cash
flow towards external capital, and rising debt issuance in particular. Third, greater
equity-market concentration increases exposure to a reassessment of technology
valuations. Together, these developments make a correction a clear risk.
To assess the potential for systemic stress, we apply the framework in Greenwood,

Hanson, Shleifer and Sørensen (GHSS). This associates heightened crisis risk with
the combination of a strong run-up in asset prices and excessive credit growth,
estimating a 45% probability of a crisis within 3 years if the two thresholds in Figure
11 are met. On a backward-looking basis as of 2025, an economy-wide assessment
finds that the equity-price increase meets the framework’s “red zone” test, but
business credit growth has been considerably more subdued, given the slowdown

### Page 11

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Deutsche Bank AG Page 11
in credit following the 2022-23 tightening cycle. That is, going into 2026, the pattern
was one of a displacement of credit from other interest-sensitive sectors towards
AI and technology rather than any broad-based rise in leverage.
Nevertheless, US data does show a clear acceleration in credit trends so far in 2026.
To make a more forward-looking assessment, we incorporate the rise in issuance

in 2026 and indicatively extend it into 2027 using the estimated rise in AI capex
plans, assuming credit growth elsewhere in the US economy remains stable
(footnote: We see two-sided risks to this – we may understate the broader credit
demand in tech adjacent areas; conversely, this may understate the drag of higher
rates on credit demand in non-AI related sectors). We also extend the estimated
2027 trend into 2028, including an assumption that equity prices continue their

trend over the past 12 months, to provide an illustrative scenario over the next two
years. Under these assumptions, we would see a visible rise in aggregate US
business sector leverage, leaving the sector just shy of economy-wide red-zone
levels by 2028. As such, approaching that territory by 2028 is conceivable if the AI
cycle continues to accelerate, both in terms of increased debt borrowing and a
renewed rise in valuations.
While the GHSS framework gives a useful quantification of the potential risks, its

indicators also warrant some qualification, which we judge to come in opposing
directions:
n On the one hand, there is a risk that aggregate credit measures may
understate vulnerabilities when borrowing is highly concentrated. Circular
financing, leases and off-balance-sheet structures complicate the picture,
and while we incorporate the recent rise in payables in our leverage metric
to capture some of this risk, it may well be underestimated. Moreover,

realised interest costs remain below current market borrowing rates after
the lengthy period of ultra-low rates, so leverage trends alone may miss the
pressure from rising debt-service costs.
n On the other hand, the price-based metric used by GHSS to assess excess
asset price growth (the 3-year real rise in equity prices) may overstate
valuation excesses given the strong rise in earnings. Indeed, P/E multiples
have already seen a sizeable correction since late 2025, which was most

pronounced for software stocks, and currently there is little evidence of
overly stretched tech valuations, especially when compared to the dot com
boom.
The risk of stress amplification is also likely to depend on the nature of any AI
correction. A downgrading of the AI capex cycle due to slower growth in AI
demand, financing constraints or supply bottlenecks may result in weaker
valuations, leading to negative wealth effects and slower capital expenditure.

However, while this would create an economic headwind, it need not produce a
systemic crisis. And while parallels to the dot com bubble may well be relevant,
currently there are factors that suggest downside risks would be more moderate.
For instance, operating cash flow still plays an important role in financing the AI
buildout, whilst there has been a limited run-up in borrowing and much less
stretched valuations relative to the late-1990s.
Our assessment therefore remains that an AI correction is a clearer risk than an AI-

led systemic crisis at this point. For these risks to become more material, it would
need leverage to keep building but AI returns to eventually disappoint, raising the
likelihood of credit events and amplifying the investment slowdown through the
real economy. We will focus on this interaction between debt-service pressures and

### Page 12

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Page 12 Deutsche Bank AG
the role of credit events more in the next section.

2. Risks from higher rates and private credit
In the previous section we noted that modest economy-wide debt growth doesn’t
necessarily imply modest credit risk when debt-service costs are rising. As existing
debt reprices or is refinanced, borrowers can face greater pressure even without
additional borrowing. This is a key feature of the debt cycle, whereby vulnerabilities
accumulate during the expansion, and rising rates (often amid a monetary

tightening due to an overheating economy) can then expose them. These risks are
relevant in the context of the current rate hiking cycle, especially as much of the
catch up in debt service costs is yet to play out (see chart below).
With banking-sector risks having moderated since the GFC, the rapid expansion of
private credit and its opaque structure have understandably drawn attention as a
possible source of the next crisis. Parallels with the subprime credit boom have

been periodically made. However, we think these should not be overstated. Even
though the dollar size of the US private credit market is similar to that of subprime
around 2006-07, the market must be assessed relative to the economy (which has
doubled in nominal terms). Moreover, the slower build-up, restrictions on
redemptions and lower risks of direct wealth effects all suggest much more
moderate amplification risks than in 2008.
There are already signs that the private-credit growth cycle is slowing (see chart)

and our credit strategists have noted warning signs including weaker new inflows
into BDCs, increasing payment-in-kind interest and prospective refinancing
pressures. That said, defaults remain limited thus far so these should not be seen
as evidence of an imminent systemic event.

### Page 14

3 November 2026
Thematic Research
Page 14 Deutsche Bank AG
Whether more systemic risks could emerge will depend on issues of solvency,
opacity and capacity for intervention.
n First, while redemption gates can limit immediate cash outflows and
reduce the need to sell illiquid loans under pressure, they do not restore a
borrower’s ability to service debt or absorb underlying credit losses. This
distinction between liquidity protection and solvency is therefore central to

judging whether a slowing credit cycle remains orderly.
n Opacity remains a concern, both in financing structures and in the
distribution of exposures among end-holders, including insurers and other
non-banks. This can make losses harder to identify and potential
amplification harder to assess.
n Policymakers’ success in containing liquidity pressures may not translate
into an effective response to credit events - liquidity provision alone cannot

repair impaired balance sheets. Extending support to opaque non-bank
exposures raises difficult questions about eligibility, loss allocation and the
threshold for intervention.
Quite aside from private credit, the fact that we are back in a rate hiking cycle from
the major central banks also adds to the current risks. Indeed, higher rates bring
down inflation, in part because they tighten financial conditions. But a
consequence of this is that it often reveals vulnerabilities in the financial system,

and makes a crisis more likely. For instance, it was a contributing factor behind the
2023 regional banking crisis, as banks had growing unrealised losses on their
securities portfolio when rates began to rise again. So even if higher rates don’t
directly cause a crisis, they can often be a key contributor. Indeed, hiking cycles
have regularly coincided with some kind of shock, as the experience of recent
decades demonstrates.

3. Stretched fiscal positions and possible NBFI amplifiers
As discussed earlier, the migration of debt towards sovereign balance sheets has

helped cushion private-sector shocks, but this has come at the cost of higher fiscal
risks. Higher public debt and sovereign yields have limited the room for fiscal
stimulus, while persistent inflation has limited the room for monetary stimulus. This
makes another round of coordinated macroeconomic support harder to deliver.
The issue with high public debt is that it if fiscal sustainability is questioned, that can
raise sovereign borrowing costs, which can amplify existing private-sector

vulnerabilities via a tightening in financial conditions and slower growth.
The financial risk posed by sovereign stress will also depend on the financial
amplification channels. These were clear in the European sovereign crisis with the
bank-sovereign loop, but the changing structure of sovereign markets suggests
that the sovereign–NBFI feedback loop may now warrant increasing attention. This
was visible with the 2022 LDI crisis in the UK, while a more recent concern is the run

up in leveraged hedge fund positions in sovereign bond markets, with hedge funds’
sovereign bond exposures more than doubling since 2022 (see chart below). As
noted also by the IMF, this reflects greater issuance and a rising share of sovereign
debt held by hedge funds.
The lessons from Black Monday, LTCM and the LDI crisis offer reassurance that
intervention can contain market stress originating outside the banking system. But
they are not a guarantee that future liquidity shocks will remain economically

benign. The greater scale and interconnectedness of non-bank finance mean that
we can’t simply rely on historical examples today.
Looking forward, a combination of sovereign repricing, leveraged financial
amplification and a more constrained policy response risks proving harder to
resolve than past episodes.

Conclusion
Whilst a major financial crisis has been avoided since the GFC and the subsequent
sovereign crisis, the problem of financial instability has yet to be permanently

solved. On the one hand, reforms to the financial system and the swift actions of
policymakers have helped to prevent a repeat. But as we know from Hyman
Minsky’s warning, lengthy periods of stability can often lay the foundations for the
next crisis, even if these take place slowly. Indeed, risks often shift rather than
disappear.
Looking forward, there are several vectors of risk worth paying attention to. But
financial crises often start in areas where people are paying little attention, or risks

are assumed to be contained. Indeed, it is the areas where risk is underappreciated
which often prove the most vulnerable.
As such, predicting where the next crisis might arise is inherently difficult. But the
topic is a vital one, given the long-lasting damage that financial crises can cause
when they do occur. Indeed, with the fiscal space for sovereigns increasingly
constrained today, the margin for a future policy error is limited.

美国中期选举后，美股将走向何方？
报告要点：
2026年美国中期选举落下帷幕，民主党赢得国会参众两院多数席位，美国政治格局由共和
党统一执政转向两党分治，特朗普政府未来在财政、税收及产业政策方面的推进将面临更多约
束。
过去几年美股上涨高度依赖 AI 产业增长预期，但随着科技巨头持续扩大资本开支，投资
回报兑现速度与融资成本之间的矛盾逐渐显现，AI相关资产可能面临由盈利预期驱动转向估值
重新定价的过程。此外，美国通胀压力仍存，货币政策宽松空间受到制约，长期利率维持高位
也对科技股估值形成约束。尽管历史上中期选举后美股往往表现较好，但当前高估值、高资本
开支及偏紧的货币环境意味着历史规律未必能够简单延续。
总体来看，美股中期走势仍需保持谨慎态度，短期不排除出现阶段性反弹，但 AI 投资回

报的不确定性及估值调整压力仍然存在。若后续科技企业盈利增长不及预期，相关板块可能面
临进一步的估值调整，其影响也可能通过市场风险偏好和指数权重向更广泛的权益市场传导。

### Page 2

宏观金融研究|股指
2026-11-10 2
美国中期选举后，美股将走向何方？
首先，美国中期选举改变政治格局，特朗普政府未来政策推进将面临更多约束。
11 月 3 日，美国中期选举落下帷幕，民主党赢得国会参众两院多数席位，美国政
治格局将由共和党同时掌控白宫与国会转向两党分治。此次共和党失利不仅受到国
内生活成本高企的影响，持续数月的伊朗战争及其推升的能源价格也加剧了选民不
满。此前，共和党在财政扩张、税收政策及产业发展方面具备相对有利的政策推进
条件，而随着民主党重新掌握国会，特朗普政府未来进一步推动减税、财政支出及
相关产业政策的难度可能上升。在外交及军事政策方面，民主党也可能通过国会监
督、军事拨款审查及战争权力决议等方式，对特朗普政府继续推进伊朗军事行动形
成约束。不过，特朗普仍保有行政权、总统否决权及较大的外交军事决策空间，两
党分治并不意味着现有政策将立即逆转，未来财政政策及伊朗局势的演变仍存在较
大不确定性。
从产业政策来看，民主党对人工智能监管的关注可能进一步影响科技板块的增长预

期。过去几年，美国政府整体倾向于推动人工智能基础设施建设，数据中心、半导
体及能源设备投资快速增长，成为支撑科技企业资本开支的重要动力。但随着数据
中心建设规模不断扩大，居民电价上涨、电网负担及能源消耗等问题逐渐引发关注。
民主党计划推动加强 AI模型安全监管、明确科技企业责任以及限制数据中心建设
对居民能源成本的影响，未来可能对相关项目审批、建设成本及投资进度形成一定
约束。尽管相关政策能否落地仍存在不确定性，但监管预期变化可能使市场重新评
估 AI基础设施投资的增长空间，进而影响半导体、算力及数据中心相关企业的盈
利预期。
值得注意的是，美股科技板块的调整早于中期选举，政治格局变化更多是本轮调整
的催化因素。10月纳斯达克指数累计下跌5.3%，而标普 500等权重指数同期上涨
5.0%，显示市场内部已经出现明显分化，此前涨幅较大的科技板块率先承压。11
月 5 日，纳斯达克指数下跌 5.52%，标普 500 指数下跌 4.36%，其中费城半导体指
数跌幅超过 12%，反映出科技板块对政策预期变化较为敏感。但从调整发生的时间

和市场结构来看，中期选举并非本轮美股下跌的根本原因，而是进一步放大了市场
对科技企业增长前景的担忧。其背后更深层次的矛盾，仍在于 AI 产业持续扩大的
投资规模能否转化为相应的盈利增长。
其次，AI 资本开支持续扩张，但投资回报兑现仍存在不确定性，科技企业面临的
经营压力逐渐显现。过去几年，美股上涨在较大程度上受益于 AI产业快速发展，
微软、谷歌、亚马逊及 Meta等科技巨头持续增加数据中心、芯片及算力基础设施
投入，推动半导体、服务器及相关产业链需求增长。然而，AI 基础设施建设具有
投资规模大、设备更新速度快、回收周期较长等特点，随着算力投入不断增加，企
业需要承担更高的设备采购、折旧及能源成本。与此同时，尽管 AI应用场景持续
拓展，但相关商业化收入能否与资本开支同步增长，仍有待进一步验证。若未来
AI 应用需求增长不及预期，持续增加的资本开支可能对企业盈利能力和现金流形
成压力。

### Page 3

宏观金融研究|股指
2026-11-10 3
从企业经营情况来看，部分科技巨头的资本开支增长已经对未来现金流预期形成影
响。10月 30 日，标普全球评级将 Meta 的评级展望由稳定调整为负面，主要原因
在于 AI 资本开支持续增长及未来自由现金流面临不确定性。Meta 预计 2026 年资
本开支将达到 1350 亿至 1450亿美元，反映出大型科技企业对 AI基础设施的投入
仍处于较高水平。对于拥有稳定经营现金流的科技巨头而言，持续扩大的资本开支
可能压缩自由现金流；而对于更加依赖外部融资的 AI数据中心企业而言，高资本
开支还可能增加债务负担与再融资压力。一旦未来需求增长放缓或融资条件收紧，
企业可能被迫调整投资计划，从而进一步影响相关产业链的增长预期。
更为重要的是，AI 产业的市场定价逻辑可能正在由关注长期增长空间转向关注实
际盈利兑现。过去，投资者主要依据 AI产业的长期发展前景给予相关企业较高估
值，资本开支持续扩张往往被视为未来收入增长的积极信号。但随着投资规模不断
扩大，市场关注点可能逐步由资本开支增速转向商业化收入、自由现金流及实际资

本回报率。即使 AI 产业的长期发展趋势没有发生根本变化，若企业盈利兑现速度
低于此前预期，科技股仍可能面临估值倍数收缩；若未来盈利增长进一步放缓，则
可能形成盈利预期下修与估值压缩的双重压力。因此，当前需要警惕的并非 AI技
术本身的发展前景，而是市场对未来增长的乐观预期可能已经较大程度地反映在股
票价格之中，实际盈利增长未必能够持续支撑现有估值水平。
最后，美国货币政策宽松空间受到制约，长期利率维持高位进一步增加了科技股的
估值压力。当前美国通胀仍具有一定黏性，经济及就业市场表现出的韧性使美联储
缺乏快速转向宽松的充分条件。11月 6日公布的美国 10月非农就业人数增加 16.5
万人，高于市场预期，进一步强化了市场对货币政策维持偏紧的判断。在这一背景
下，美国长期国债收益率仍处于较高水平，对于依赖未来现金流增长支撑估值的科
技企业而言，较高的贴现率将降低未来盈利的现值，从而限制估值扩张。与此同时，
融资成本上升也可能增加 AI基础设施建设的财务负担，使部分依赖外部融资的企
业面临更大的经营压力。因此，科技股不仅需要面对自身盈利兑现的考验，还受到

外部利率和融资环境的共同约束。
总体来看，美股中期走势仍需保持谨慎态度，AI 投资回报的不确定性与高利率环
境下的估值调整压力仍然存在。短期来看，前期科技板块经历明显调整后，不排除
市场出现阶段性反弹，但持续修复仍需要企业盈利及货币政策提供进一步支撑。中
期来看，若科技企业盈利增长不及预期，或 AI基础设施投资出现放缓，相关板块
可能面临进一步估值调整，并通过大型科技股的指数权重及全球风险偏好向更广泛
的权益市场传导。对 A 股而言，美股科技板块调整可能影响国内 AI产业链的增长
预期及相关资产估值，但国内经济基本面、流动性及产业政策仍是决定市场中期走
势的重要因素。未来需要重点关注 AI资本开支与盈利增长的匹配程度、美联储货
币政策变化以及中期选举后相关政策的实际落地情况，以判断美股后续调整的持续
性与潜在影响。

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 建材 ‧ 台灣

億豐 
(8464.TW /8464 TT)

2027 年獲利將維持成長 
重要訊息 
億豐 3Q26 EPS 為 6.38 元，低於預期 7.8%。 
評論及分析  
IEEPA 退稅認列金額較 預期低，億豐 3Q26 稅後淨利低於預期 7.8%。億豐
3Q26 營收 84.8 億元、YoY+15.9% ，優於我們原先預期 9.5%，來自產線人
力補足後，固定式補 出貨，以及非美地區客製化成長強勁。毛利率部分，
IEEPA 關稅退稅認列於成本減項，挹注毛利率約 2 個百分點至 60.8%，抵銷
固定式佔比增加對毛利率的稀釋，營業利益 24.7 億元、YoY+15.9%，優於
預期 6.5% 。業外認列匯損約 7000 萬元，稅後淨利 18.7 億元、
YoY+9.6% ，EPS 為 6.38 元，低於預期 7.8%。低於預期為 IEEPA 退稅認列
差異及匯損，我們原先預期退稅將認列於業外收益，金額為 4 億元，不過財
報將此認列為成本減項 分於 2H26 認列，3Q26 單季認列金額約 1.7 億元。 
總體環境逆風預期減緩，新產品出貨及市佔擴張， 預估 2027 年獲利可維持
成長。今年以來美國房市持續低迷影響窗飾市場 ，油價及海運價格大漲也對
億豐成本及費用造成壓力，還原 IEEPA 關稅退稅影響後，累計前三季億豐本
業獲利年減約 1.4%。展望 2027 年，美國期中選舉共和黨失利後，市場已
開始預期川普對於 關稅戰及美伊戰爭的態度可能更加緩和，而財政紀律恢復
也有助利率下降 ，整體而言，我們認為 2027 年總體環境對億豐將較為友
善。另外， 2027 年億豐固定式將開始出貨電動及免打孔系列新 產品，客製
化部分，得益於生產效率提升，億豐旗下 NORMAN、VENETTA 與同業價差
已由去年之 30%放大至 30~40%，有助市占進一步擴張。 凱基預估 2027
年億豐 EPS 為 25.8 元、YoY +9.9%。 
投資建議  
我們維持對億豐正向看法及「增加持股」投資評等，目標價調降至 439 元，
基於 2027 年 EPS 之 17 倍評價而得，位於歷史本益比區間中緣 。 
投資風險  
聯準會利率變化、油價、美國房市政策 。 
增加持股‧維持

圖 3: 公司概況  圖 4: 高客製化產品營收佔比持續提升 
1974 年成立的億豐是全球最大的固定尺寸窗簾製造商 ，市佔
率達 50-60%。客製化框型百葉窗 /捲簾為公司營收大宗，佔比
達 73%，另外 24%則由固定尺寸產品貢獻，其主要是透過美
國大型零售商，包括 Home Depot 與 Walmart 銷售。美國市
場是公司最主要的營收來源，營收比重為 69%，其次是歐洲
市場的 25%。公司的產線主要位於中國、柬埔寨與墨西哥。

台灣 億豐

七月 03, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 5

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、
生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
約當二氧化碳排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合
物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

單位營收碳排量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合
物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收比例 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收比例 
- 廢棄物回收比例 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

耗水量 
耗水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性主管 
女性主管百分比 
- 女性主管占公司主管的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性主管之百分比 
- 女性主管百分比 = 女性主管人數/主管總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發
展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

## Document 657: 叫停万斯与伊朗谈判，特朗普“180度转变”：转向“不战不谈”

Source PDF: all report pdf/叫停万斯与伊朗谈判，特朗普“180度转变”：转向“不战不谈”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

特朗普淡化全⾯外交解决⽅案的前景，转⽽接受 " 不战不谈 " 僵局。共和党在上周中期选举中
的⼴泛失利，是特朗普此次政策转向的直接背景。选举结果凸显了伊朗战事在普通美国选
⺠中的⾼度不受欢迎。分析⼈⼠警告，这反映出美国已陷⼊⽆好选项的困境。伊朗迟早会
主动打破僵局。
摘要：
据新华社报道，美国总统特朗普 12 ⽇在社交媒体上称，已要求副总统万斯把全部注意⼒集中在
美国各地⺠主党执政城市和州 “ 正在发⽣的⼤规模腐败与犯罪 ” 上，并称美国不需要与伊朗达成协
议。这⼀表态标志着特朗普对伊政策出现重⼤转向。
当地时间 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇，特朗普称，“我们不需要与伊朗达成任何协议，那些⽇⼦早已过去”，同时
叫停万斯正在推进的美伊谈判进程，放弃此前坚持的以核协议为⽬标的战争逻辑，转⽽接受⼀
种 “ 不战不谈 ” 的僵局状态。这是特朗普在伊朗问题上⼜⼀次急剧转向 —— 此前他曾多次威胁称若
⽆法达成协议将发动新⼀轮打击。
上述转变发⽣在共和党于上周中期选举中遭遇重挫之后。分析⼈⼠指出，伊朗战事的⾼度不受

欢迎是共和党失利的重要因素之⼀，这迫使特朗普在升级军事⾏动与维持现状之间寻找第三条
路。油价在特朗普发表上述评论后持续⾛⾼，布伦特原油周四收涨 3.3% ，报每桶约 96 美元，反
映出市场对波斯湾地缘政治紧张局势将持续的预期。
万斯谈判进程戛然⽽⽌
万斯此前深度介⼊美伊谈判进程。据新华社报道，今年 4 ⽉，万斯率美国谈判团队参加在巴基斯
坦⾸都伊斯兰堡举⾏的美伊谈判；美伊 6 ⽉达成谅解备忘录后，万斯⼜率团参加在瑞⼠⽐尔根⼭
举⾏的美伊谈判。
就在特朗普发出上述社交媒体帖⽂前数⽇，谈判仍在积极推进。据美媒 11 ⽉ 6 ⽇报道，万斯过去
两周直接参与了与伊朗的间接会谈，寻求与伊朗达成新的停⽕协议并重启核谈判。⽽据媒体 11
⽇报道，万斯正考虑与伊朗最⾼国家安全委员会秘书雷扎伊举⾏⾯对⾯会谈，以推动结束伊朗
战事，⽽特朗普对此持开放态度并已授权其开展谈判⼯作。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

然⽽特朗普随即急转直下，在社交媒体上宣布叫停这⼀进程，并要求万斯将 “ 全部精⼒集中在⺠
主党执政的城市与州的严重腐败和犯罪问题上 ” 。值得注意的是，华尔街⻅闻⽂章此前写道，万
斯本⼈在 11 ⽉ 4 ⽇曾发表⻓⽂，直⾔伊朗战事是共和党中期选举失利的根源所在，被美国媒体分
析认为包含对特朗普 " 不加掩饰的批评 " 。
中期选举失利，政策转向的直接导⽕索
据媒体报道，共和党在上周中期选举中的⼴泛失利，是特朗普此次政策转向的直接背景。选举
结果凸显了伊朗战事在普通美国选⺠中的⾼度不受欢迎，令特朗普陷⼊两难困境：要么重新升
级打击⾏动，要么按兵不动、继续加⼤对伊朗的经济施压，寄望于未来获得让步。
特朗普现在选择了第三条路 ——淡化全⾯外交解决⽅案的前景，转⽽接受僵局。他近期在公开
场合多次刻意淡化冲突的规模、持续时间和烈度，称冲突 “ 实际上已经结束 ” ，并表⽰ “ 我们赢
了 ” ，同时强调 “ 已经好⼏个⽉没有交⽕了，这算不上什么战争 ” 。
然⽽，中东地区的实际局势与这⼀表述存在明显落差。据报道，周四伊朗军⽅宣布，两艘 “ 不合

规 ” 油轮在霍尔⽊兹海峡附近触雷起⽕。⻛险咨询机构 Verisk Maplecroft 的报告显⽰，上⽉针对
商业船只的确认攻击事件达 36 起，为战事爆发以来单⽉最⾼，并预测伊朗对船只的骚扰⾏动将
持续⾄ 2027 年。与此同时，财政部⻓⻉森特因受到伊朗威胁，已被转移⾄安全军事住所，另有
约六名特朗普政府⾼级官员同样被迫转移。
⾯对外界质疑，⽩宫官员坚持认为美国⽬前仍处于有利地位。据报道，⼀名⽩宫官员表⽰，海
军封锁、持续扩⼤的制裁⾏动，以及⼀项名为 “铁⻔⾏动”（ Operation Iron Gate ）的新遏制⾏动
—— 聚焦伊朗陆地边境与⾥海沿岸 —— 正在对伊朗形成⽇益增⼤的压⼒。该官员重申，特朗普
政府将确保伊朗永远⽆法获得核武器。
然⽽，这⼀策略本⾝也⾯临诸多不确定性。前美国官员等⼈⼠认为，美国⽆法⽆限期维持对伊
朗的压⼒，⽽伊朗⾃⾝也拥有反制选项，包括对美国在该地区的军事基地或盟友发动军事升级
⾏动。
“ 没有好选项 ” ：三条路均存隐患
多名分析⼈⼠对特朗普的这⼀转向提出了警⽰。曾在奥巴⻢政府参与伊朗核协议谈判、并在特

朗普第⼀任期参与 “ 极限施压 ” 战略制定的前美国官员 Nate Swanson 表⽰， “这反映出政府已陷⼊
⽆好选项的困境 —— 既没有可⾏的军事路径，⼜⾯临升级⻛险；也没有达成⼀项好协议的真实
前景。 ”
国际危机组织伊朗问题专家 Ali Vaez 则指出， “ 华盛顿可以选择维持现状，但⽆法强迫德⿊兰⽆
限期接受这⼀现状 —— 伊朗迟早会尝试打破封锁。 ” 他警告称，美国官员 “ 押注压⼒最终将带来伊
朗屈服或政权崩溃这⼀难以企及的⽬标，但危险在于，这可能先带来另⼀场战争 ” 。
⺠主保卫基⾦会伊朗项⽬⾼级主任 Behnam Ben Taleblu 则表⽰，特朗普叫停对协议的追求是正
确的，但 “ ⽬前对伊朗并没有任何管控⽅案 ” 。
据报道，⼀名熟悉海湾国家⾸都决策层思路的⼈⼠表⽰，特朗普放弃战争⽬标、可能在没有核
协议的情况下就此收⼿，将使波斯湾伙伴国在⾯对⼀个更加强硬的伊朗时缺乏明确的稳定路
径。
海湾阿拉伯国家或将尝试通过将伊朗纳⼊某种地区协议的⽅式来平息冲突，但特朗普也可能最

终否决阿拉伯国家联盟或海湾合作委员会与伊朗谈判达成的任何协议。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

維持評等  
增加持股(Overweight) 
2027 年雙位數成長，惟毛利率展望 較為保守  
⚫ 3Q26 財報優於預期， 2027 年目標雙位數成長  
⚫ Switch 業務未來 2~3年成長率 70~90%  
⚫ 缺料影響仍需時間緩解，毛利率目標 12%  
3Q26 財報優於預期 ：啟碁 3Q26 營收 487 .0 億元 (QoQ+ 23 %，
YoY+ 78 %)，毛利率 12.2%(QoQ -0.7 7ppts)，主因轉嫁原料漲價成
本稀釋毛利率 ，低於我們先前預期 13.0 %，費用率因營收規模 擴
大下降 至 6.96 %，優於預期 ，EPS 4.32 元優於市場共識 4.07 元。
三大產品線需求 均有所成長 ， Service Provider 成長力道最為強
勁，主要營運商、電信商客戶均接近翻倍成長 。  
新產品持續推出， 2027/2028 年維持成長 格局 ：公司表示
2027/2028 年整體維持成長格局，主要成長動能來自新產品，
800G Switch 產品於 3Q26 推出，雖然 2026 年僅達成內部目標低
標，然公司預期未來 2~3 年有望有 70~90% 成長率，而 1.6T 交換
器、 OCS 等預期 2027 年中送樣，目標 2028 年貢獻營收 。整體
而言公司朝營收 60億美元目標發展， 2027 年目標雙位數成長。  
維持積極資本支出 ：啟碁 2026 年 CAPEX 約 30億元，主要用於
越南產能擴充， 2H26 越南產能 較便年翻倍 ，預期 2027 年 CAPEX
將維持高水準， 主要用於 越南、美國產能 擴充、自動化等，公司
先前預 期越南廠擴建後 2027 年可達成 1,700~1,800 億元產能規
模。而美國設廠則在計畫中，最快預期 2028 年開始生產。  
缺料影響仍需時間緩解 ：公司毛利率表現低於市場共識，主
因原物料成本持續漲價 ，且公司未必能及時反映漲價給客戶。
其中公司預期 2027 年記憶體漲價相對趨緩，但缺料仍可能影
響產品交貨，預期 2027 年仍難以緩解，公司 目標維持 12%以
上毛利率。  
評價：考量公司毛利率展望低於預期，且 Switch 業務仍需時
間發酵，我們下修本益比評價 ，維持「增加持股」 評等，目標
價元 215 元 (202 7年 EPS x 13倍 PER)。風險因子： LEO 供應
比重低於預期 、 Switch 業務成長性不如預期 。

王翊安 
wade.wang@ctbcsis.com

ABF載板 
欣興 (3037 TT)

重新覆蓋  
買進(Buy) 
欣興向榮， ABF 漲價啟動  
⚫ 規格與出貨同步升級， 2027年 ABF漲幅上看 30% 
⚫ 新廠逐年開出， ABF產能年增逾三成  
⚫ 切入 Intel EMIB 平台，帶動 ABF載板單位價值提升  
3Q26 營收創高， BT 轉產暫壓獲利： 欣興 3Q26 營收 526.5 億元
(QoQ+22.8%)，毛利率 28.9%(QoQ+4.1ppts) ，稅後淨利 100.5億元，EPS 
6.36 元。整體獲利低於市場預期，主因有兩個。 (1)台灣 BT廠區逐步
轉換至 ABF，部分產線停工，稼動率較 2Q26下滑，導致 BT毛利率僅
低個位數；3Q26又調整員工分紅比例，影響毛利率約 1.2ppts。(2)2Q26
業外認列較多金融資產評價利益，墊高獲利基期， 3Q26稅後淨利 QoQ-
23.4%。展望 4Q26，由於上游原料持續 維持緊缺格局，預期 BT報價將
續漲中個位數， ABF報價 QoQ+10%，ABF毛利率則往 50%以上看待。
預估 4Q26 營收達 655.4 億元 (QoQ+24.5%, YoY+88.9%) ，毛利率
32.7%(QoQ+3.9ppts) ，EPS 8.36 元。  
規格與出貨同步升級， 2027 年 ABF 缺口擴大： 欣興為目前 AI 
GPU/ASIC 主要的載板供應商， 2H26高階 ABF已占 ABF營收 70%以
上。展望 2027年，NVIDIA Rubin 、Google TPU v8 等大型 GPU/ASIC
方案放量，出貨顆數上升，封裝層數與面積亦持續提高。以 NVIDIA 為
例，載板層數由 B200的16~20L 提升至 Rubin的18~24L，尺寸放大至
100x100mm，ASP提升 2~3倍。我們預估在出貨量與規格同步提升下，
2027年ABF載板供需缺口將擴大至 30%以上；加上 T-glass 等關鍵原
料2027年仍供不應求，預期欣興 2027年ABF全年漲幅可達 30%，推
升ABF全年平均毛利率至 50%以上。  
漲價與擴產並進，獲利成長明確： 2027年需求將帶動 ABF漲價循環，
我們預期載板廠將開始把產能緊缺反映在報價上，毛利擴張速度將顯
著優於 2026年。欣興 2027~2028 年產能開出進度明確，並以客戶預付
款支應擴產資金， 考慮擴產以及 BT 產能逐漸轉往 ABF，預估欣興
2026~2028 年ABF產能將分別成長 37.2%/36%/36% 。我們對公司後續
成長保持樂觀。預估欣興 2026~2028 年 EPS分別為 26.35/52.61/86.97
元；以2027年EPS及25倍本益比推估目標價為 1,315元，建議買進。 
風險因子： AI晶片需求放緩、 EMIB良率不如預期、漲價受限 LTA。

2 
 公司簡介：欣興成立於 1990 年，為聯電集團旗下的 PCB與 IC載板廠。公 司生產基地以台灣為主，並於中國、日本、德國設有據點，泰國廠為 HDI新
產能；近年陸續處分效益不佳的海外子公司，資源集中於台灣 ABF擴產。產
品線涵蓋 IC載板、HDI、PCB及FPC，3Q26載板營收占比達 62%(其中 ABF
占55%)，HDI及PCB分別占 27%及9%。欣興為全球最大 IC載板廠，高階
ABF載板供應僅次於 Ibiden，具備載板、HDI至PCB的完整製程能力；截至
2026年3月底，集團員工約 31,000 人。  
圖表一、欣興營收產品組合趨勢

資料來源： Bloomberg、中信投顧整理

GPU/ASIC 出貨顆數大幅成長： 2H26~1H27 是各大 GPU/ASIC 廠商推出新專
案的階段， 2027 年整體出貨量將較 2026 年顯著提升。我們預估 2027 年
NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU 及AWS Trainium 出貨量將分別達 800萬、900萬
及 300萬顆以上，帶動整體 GPU/ASIC 出貨量 YoY+55%。即使不考慮規格
升級，單看出貨顆數成長，亦將推升 ABF載板需求。

3 
封裝規格提升帶動載板用量增加： 除出貨量成長外，新產品的規格升級將進
一步增加 ABF載板用量。以 NVIDIA GPU 為例，載板尺寸由 Hopper 世代的
55x55mm 放大至 Rubin Ultra 的 120x120mm 以上，層數亦由 12L 增加至
24~30L。在不考慮良率的情況下，單顆晶片的 ABF消耗量增加約 10~12倍，
單顆 content value 亦提升 4倍以上。ASIC 亦呈現相同的規格升級趨勢。我
們根據晶片出貨顆數及單顆晶片所需 ABF面積的成長推估， 2027年ABF供
需缺口將擴大至 30%以上，帶動 2027年ABF漲幅上看 30%。 
ABF 將是欣興未來兩年的成長主軸。我們預估欣興 2027/2028 年 ABF 營收
占比將達 62.6%/72.1%；在漲價、良率穩定及稼動率滿載下，預估 2027/2028
年 ABF毛利率將達 51.3%/54.4%，符合公司 ABF毛利率 50%以上、長期上
看60%的指引。

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                       2 0 2 6 / 11 / 06

4 
BT漲價邏輯在於產能排擠： 過去市場認為 BT漲價力道不如 ABF，主因在
於消費性需求疲弱。 2026年消費性需求雖仍疲弱，但記憶體需求強勁，填補
了消費性的缺口。供給面來看， BT過去幾年表現疲弱，大廠不敢大幅擴產；
加上欣興等大廠將產能逐步分配給利潤更好的 ABF載板，自 2026年起逐步
減產 BT、將產能移轉至 ABF。然而產能移轉期間，欣興 BT毛利率持續受
到壓抑，3Q26 BT 毛利率僅低個位數。  
此外，上游 T-glass 供應仍然緊缺。根據供應鏈調查， T-glass 目前約 70%需
求來自 ABF載板、30%來自 BT；供給端 Nittobo 約占 40%，其餘 60%由台
灣及中國廠商供應。 Nittobo 雖於 2027年陸續開出 T-glass新產能，但增幅有
限，加上 2027年ABF需求大幅成長，我們預期供需缺口將延續至 2028年。
因此，即使 BT需求成長幅度不及 ABF，在(1)產能排擠及 (2)上游原料供給緊
缺未解下，我們預估 BT 2027 年仍有 15~20%的漲幅。

欣興將為 EMIB 主要載板供應商之一： CoWoS 與 EMIB 皆為將運算晶片與
HBM 整合於同一封裝內的 2.5D 先進封裝技術。兩者差異在於， CoWoS 為
「晶片 →中介層 →載板」的三層結構：晶片先置於台積電製作的中介層
(interposer) 上，晶片間的細線路全部由中介層完成，再將中介層置於 ABF載
板上。此架構下，載板僅負責承接中介層，並將訊號與電力傳導至 PCB，屬
被動承載角色。 EMIB 則移除中介層，晶片直接置於載板上，僅在兩顆晶片
交界下方的載板增層中埋入小塊矽橋 (Silicon Bridge) 完成高密度互連；
EMIB-T再於矽橋中加入矽穿孔 (TSV)，使電力與訊號可垂直穿過矽橋，以支
援更大尺寸封裝與 HBM4。由於不需大面積中介層， EMIB 封裝尺寸不受中
介層面積限制，成本較低，但互連僅發生於晶片邊緣，頻寬不及整片中介層。  
圖表六、EMIB平台對比 CoWoS示意圖

資料來源：中信投顧整理  
對載板廠而言， EMIB的content value 較高，原因在於原本由中介層承擔的
部分功能移轉至載板端，載板廠須在增層中挖槽、精準對位埋入矽橋，並於
大尺寸下維持平整度與翹曲控制，等同將部分封裝工序內化至載板製程中，
故欣興於 EMIB平台的單價較 CoWoS更高。  
但良率仍為主要瓶頸之一，目前欣興 EMIB 載板良率目標為 50%；EMIB-T
因內埋設計更多，製程難度更高，預計 2027 年開始初步試產，設備大量移
入(move-in)時程則視第一階段試產的良率表現而定。供應商方面，目前 EMIB
載板供應商含欣興約 5家，EMIB-T約3家。欣興預計於楊梅廠配置約 10~15%
產能支援 EMIB載板生產，良率與日系同業相當。

### Page 5

個股報告  
                                       2 0 2 6 / 11 / 06

5 
圖表七、Ibiden 載板 Roadmap

資料來源： Ibiden

目前預計導入 EMIB設計的專案以 Google TPU v9 為主，其 reticle size 達9
倍。在出貨量龐大、 EMIB 良率仍低的情況下，預期將大量消耗載板產能，
以初期良率 50%估算，代表一半的產能與材料在製程中耗損，有效產出偏低。
綜合上述因素，我們預期高階 ABF 載板缺貨將持續兩年以上，成為推升載
板供不應求與漲價的因素之一。加上 EMIB的content value 較高，即使EMIB
短期內對欣興獲利的直接貢獻有限，仍有助於推升欣興 ABF平均單價。

新廠逐年開出， ABF 產能年增逾三成： 產能方面，欣興規劃每年新增一座
ABF廠區。光復二廠分三期開出， 2Q27開出一期、2H27開出二期，三期最
快 2027 年底開出；楊梅二廠預計 2028 年底開出，楊梅三廠已動工，預計
2029年量產。以每座新廠初期月產能約 7,000片為基準，每增加一座廠區可
貢獻約 30~40%產能，且楊梅二、三廠規模將大於光復一、二廠。資本支出
方面，公司將 2026年資本支出由 197億元上修至 537億元，10月董事會再
追加 509億元長交期設備採購，對應 2027~2028 年產能建置。客戶預付款已
足以支應未來兩年擴產所需，加上台灣廠區 BT產能逐步轉作 ABF，我們預
估欣興 2026/2027/2028 年ABF產能將分別成長 37.2%/36%/36% 。

整體而言，欣興在本波 ABF載板漲價循環中具備足夠的擴產幅度，並於 AI 
GPU/ASIC ABF 載板市場握有主要份額，我們預估欣興 2026/2027/2028 年
EPS分別為 26.58/52.61/86.97 元。此外，欣興為台系載板廠中投入 EMIB、
玻璃載板等新技術最積極的廠商，有助於鞏固中長期競爭地位。以 2027 年
EPS及25倍本益比推估目標價為 1,315 元，建議買進。

## Document 740: 欧盟拟立法规范网络交易行为，违规企业最高面临全球营收6%罚款

Source PDF: all report pdf/欧盟拟立法规范网络交易行为，违规企业最高面临全球营收6%罚款.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

欧盟委员会提出《数字公平法案》草案，禁⽌欺骗性界⾯设计、动态定价及针对⼉童的个
性化⼴告等不公平⽹络交易⾏为，违规企业最⾼将被处以全球年营业额 6% 的罚款。法案还
监管游戏成瘾性内购、 " 订阅陷阱 " 及⽹红商业透明度。然⽽，该草案尚需成员国和欧洲议会
批准，且遭业界批评存在监管重叠、削弱法律确定性等问题。
摘要：
欧盟委员会提出《数字公平法案》草案，剑指欺骗性界⾯设计、动态定价及针对⼉童的个性化
⼴告等不公平⽹络交易⾏为，违规企业将⾯临全球年度总营业额最⾼ 6% 的巨额罚款。
11 ⽉ 11 ⽇周三，欧盟司法专员 Michael McGrath 在新闻发布会上表⽰：
他强调，⽴法⽬标并⾮增加正当经营的难度，⽽是提⾼不公平经营的代价。
该草案还附带⼀项对《消费者保护合作条例》的修订计划，将赋予欧盟委员会调查跨成员国重
⼤案件的权⼒，同样可对违规企业处以最⾼全球年收⼊ 6% 的罚款。
不过，该法案在正式成为法律前，仍需在未来数⽉内获得欧盟各成员国及欧洲议会批准。
禁⽌ " 暗⿊模式 " ，剑指成瘾性设计

据路透社报道，《数字公平法案》明确将欺骗性界⾯设计列为禁⽌对象，此类设计利⽤⼼理诱导
⼿段驱使消费者超额⽀出。
法案同时监管游戏中的 “ 氪⾦获胜 ” （ pay-to-win ）与 “ 付费加速 ” （ pay-to-progress ）等成瘾性内
购功能。监管机构认为，这些机制极易诱发过度消费，对未成年⼈群体的危害尤为显著。
此外，法案要求企业必须为⽤⼾提供关闭数字产品成瘾性设计的选项，并要求⽹红博主对商业
合作内容保持更⾼透明度，以保障消费者的知情权。
法案还将⽭头指向动态定价机制。这⼀基于实时需求波动调整票价的定价模式，近年来已引发
⼤量消费者投诉。
与此同时，法案将对 " 订阅陷阱 " 进⾏规范化。欧盟委员会估计，此类陷阱每年给欧洲消费者造成
数⼗亿欧元的损失。
作为更⼤范围整顿⾏动的组成部分，《数字公平法案》要求科技巨头和在线平台加强平台⾃律与
合规管理，监管触⻆进⼀步延伸⾄数字消费全链条。
业界批评草案存在监管重叠
报道指出，该⽴法草案亦⾯临来⾃企业界的质疑。
我们的消费者保护规则将确保企业凭借产品和服务的优势进⾏竞争，⽽⾮依靠剥削消费者的能

⼒。

2
◆ドル円相場回顧
９月のドル円相場は、上旬に一時152円台後半
へドル安・円高が進行。日銀高官によるタカ派的な
発言をきっかけに、日銀による利上げペース加速への
思惑が強まったほか 、米政府高官による円売りけん
制発言などが円を押し上げ 。この間、投機筋による
円売りポジションが急速に巻き戻されたことも円高に
寄与。
下旬にかけて、159円台前半へドル高・円安が進
行。市場予想を上回る米経済指標を受け、ＦＲＢ
による早期利上げ観測が強まったことが背景。
10月入り後は、158円台前半を中心に横ばい圏
で推移。日銀が公表した「金融政策決定会合にお
ける主な意見」を受けて、日銀の早期利上げ観測が
後退した一方、米雇用統計が市場予想を下回り 、

ＦＲＢによる早期利上げ観測も後退し、方向感が
定まらない展開に。
◆今後の見通し
当面のドル円相場は、横ばい圏で推移する見通し。
資源高によるインフレ懸念の高まりからＦＲＢ・日銀
ともに利上げを継続し 、米日金利差が大きく変わら
ないとみられることが背景。
来春以降は、緩やかにドル安・円高が進行する見
通し。ＦＲＢは利上げによる景気や物価への影響
を見極めるため、政策金利を据え置く一方、日銀は
来年半ばにかけて四半期に一度のペースで利上げ
を継続し、米日金利差が縮小することが背景。
ドル円相場見通し：来春以降、緩やかな円高へ

為替
(株)日本総合研究所 為替相場展望 2026年10月
◆ユーロ相場回顧
９月のユーロドル相場は、上旬に1.16ドル台前半
を中心に横ばい圏で推移 。ユーロ圏のインフレ率が
前月から上昇した一方、米雇用統計が市場予想を
上回り、ＥＣＢ・ＦＲＢともに早期利上げ観測が
強まったことが背景。その後、10月上旬にかけて、一
時1.11ドル台半ばへユーロ安・ドル高が進行。堅調
な米経済指標がドル高圧力となったほか 、フランスの
財政不安がユーロ安圧力に。
対円では、上旬に日銀高官によるタカ派的な発言
を受けて、177円台後半へ円高が進行 。中旬には、
日銀の早期利上げ観測が後退したことから 、一時
181円台半ばへ円安が進行。その後、10月上旬に
かけてフランスの予算編成を巡る財政悪化懸念から 、
一時176円台前半へユーロが下落。
◆今後の見通し
先行きユーロドル相場は 、緩やかなユーロ安基調
で推移する見通し。当面はフランスの財政不安が重
石となるほか、来春にかけてＥＣＢは政策金利を据
え置く一方、ＦＲＢは追加利上げを実施するとみら

れることが背景。もっとも、来年半ば以降は利上げが
打ち止めとなり、横ばい圏で推移する見込み。
対円では、緩やかなユーロ安基調で推移する見通
し。当面はフランスの財政悪化懸念がユーロの重石
となるほか、来年半ばにかけては 、日銀の段階的な
追加利上げに伴う日欧金利差の縮小が円高圧力
に。
ユーロ相場見通し：緩やかなユーロ安へ

◆過去の選挙後、ドル円の方向はまちまち
11月に実施される米中間選挙を控え 、下院は民
主党が優勢との予測が大勢。民主党が過半数を奪
還した場合、政権と議会多数派が異なる 「分割政
府」となる見込み 。過去10回の中間選挙 でも、分
割政府は９回と大半。
もっとも、過去の選挙後のドル円相場はドル高 ・ド
ル安の双方へと推移しており 、一定の傾向はみられ
ず。選挙結果だけから、先行きのドル円相場の方向
性を見通すことは困難。
◆方向性を左右するのは日米金融政策
先行きのドル円相場を見通すうえでは 、日米金融
政策が重要な要因のひとつ 。中間選挙後に顕著に
円高が進んだ 2022年も、米ＣＰＩの下振れを受

けたＦＲＢの利上げペース減速観測や 、日銀の
イールドカーブ ・コントロール政策の修正などにより 、
米日金利差が縮小したことが背景。
当面のドル円相場は 、足元の円安圏で推移する
見通し。来春にかけて、資源高によるインフレ懸念の
高まりからＦＲＢ・日銀ともに利上げを継続するとみ
られ、米日金利差が大きく変わらないことが背景。

米中対立の常態化や米国の自国第一主義の強まりを背景に、既存の国際経済秩序が揺らいでいます。こうしたなか、各国は存
在感を高めるグローバルサウス諸国との外交・経済関係の強化を急いでいます。日本も、OECD（経済協力開発機構）などと協調し
ながら、関係強化を目指していますが、現状はASEANやインドなどに偏重しており、アフリカや中南米との連携は限定的です。本シン
ポジウムでは、はじめに世界経済の構造変化や日本のグローバルサウス諸国との連携策の現状・課題を多角的に検証します。その
上で、日本がグローバルサウス諸国との関係を深化させるための具体的な戦略を議論します。

2
◆ドル円相場回顧
10月のドル円相場は 、中旬にかけて158円台前
半を中心に横ばい圏で推移 。米国では、消費者物
価指数の伸びが市場予想を上回った一方 、日本で
は、日銀高官によるタカ派的な発言など、ドル高と円
高双方の材料が入り交じったことが背景。
下旬には、米ＡＩ関連企業の収益性をめぐる不
透明感の高まりから 、投資家によるリスク回避の円
買いが強まり、一時156円台前半へ円高が進行。
もっとも、11月初にかけては、堅調な米経済指標
を背景に、158円台後半へドルが反発 。その後は、
米中間選挙での民主党の勝利を受けて、米政府の
財政悪化懸念が後退し 、米長期金利が低下 。米
日金利差が縮小したことで 、一時157円台前半へ

ドルが下落するなど振れの大きい展開。
◆今後の見通し
当面のドル円相場は、横ばい圏で推移する見通し。
資源高によるインフレ懸念の高まりなどを背景に 、Ｆ
ＲＢ・日銀ともに利上げを継続し 、米日金利差が
大きく変わらないとみられることが背景。
来春以降は、緩やかにドル安・円高が進行する見
通し。ＦＲＢは利上げによる景気や物価への影響
を見極めるため、政策金利を据え置く一方、日銀は
来年半ばにかけて四半期に一度のペースで利上げ
を継続し、米日金利差が縮小することが背景。
ドル円相場見通し：来春以降、緩やかな円高へ

◆ユーロ相場回顧
10月のユーロドル相場は 、上旬に一時1.11ドル
台後半へユーロが下落 。フランスの財政不安の高ま
りが背景。月末にかけては、1.11ドル台前半へユー
ロが再び下落 。フランスの政治不安の強まりや 、軟
調なユーロ圏の経済指標などが背景。この間、投機
筋によるユーロ売りポジションは拡大 。11月初には、
一時1.12ドル台後半へドル安が進行 。米中間選
挙での民主党の勝利を受けた米長期金利の低下
などが背景。もっとも、その後は堅調な米雇用統計を
受けて、1.11ドル台前半へドルが反発。
対円では、フランスの財政悪化懸念から 、上旬に
176円台前半へユーロが下落 。下旬には、フランス
の予算審議を巡る不透明感などから175円台前半
へユーロが続落 。11月初にはＥＣＢ高官のタカ派
的な発言から、178円台後半へユーロが反発。
◆今後の見通し
先行きのユーロドル相場は、緩やかなユーロ安が続
く見通し。当面はフランスの財政不安がユーロの重

石となる見込み。また、ＥＣＢは年明け以降、政策
金利を据え置く一方、ＦＲＢは来年にも追加利上
げを実施する見込み。その後、両中銀ともに政策金
利を据え置くと予想され横ばい圏で推移する見込み。
対円では、緩やかなユーロ安基調で推移する見通
し。当面はフランスの財政悪化懸念がユーロの重石
となるほか、来年半ばにかけては 、日銀の段階的な
利上げに伴う日欧金利差の縮小が円高圧力に。
ユーロ相場見通し：緩やかなユーロ安へ

為替
(株)日本総合研究所 為替相場展望 2026年11月4
◆フランスの財政不安がユーロの重石に
９月中旬以降、フランスの財政・政治不安の高ま
りを受けて、ユーロ安が進行 。９月には、フランス政
府が2026年の財政赤字目標未達を表明 。10月
初には、540億ユーロ規模の財政再建策を盛り込
んだ2027年予算案を公表したものの、議会の反対
により審議が難航。財政再建策への反発 、物価高
や公共サービスなどへの不満を背景に 、各地でデモ
やストライキが発生 。仏独10年金利差は 2010年
代前半の欧州債務危機以来の水準へ拡大。
当面、フランスの財政再建を巡る不透明感がユー
ロの重石となる見込み 。フランス政府は、2029年ま
でに財政赤字をＧＤＰ比３％以内に縮小すること
を目指す一方 、赤字削減は大統領選後の 2028
～29年に偏る構図。過去にも是正期限が延長され
た経緯があり、目標達成は不透明。また、財政再建
を巡る議会内の対立は根強く 、11月には修正後の
予算案が否決されるなど 、予算案の成立には時間

を要する見込み。
◆仏大統領選がさらなるユーロ安リスクに
さらに、来春のフランス大統領選挙後 、財政不安
が一段と高まることで、ユーロが下落するリスクに注意
が必要。支持率首位の極右政党・国民連合のルペ
ン氏は、他国との調整を要するＥＵ拠出金の削減
などの財政再建策を掲げる一方 、大規模減税も主
張。歳出削減が計画を下回るなかで 、減税が実施
されれば、財政赤字拡大が計画を上回る可能性。
トピック：フランスの財政・政治不安が重石となるユーロ相場

Asia Trends

豪ドル相場に持ち直しの兆し、RBA による追加利上げ観測が再燃 
～住宅市場の調整が懸念される一方、根強いインフレ圧力が金融引き締め継続を後押し～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ９月以降の豪ドルは弱含んできた。ＦＲＢが９月に利上げを決定して追加利上げ観測が強ま
ったことに加え、ＲＢＡも９月に利上げを再開したものの、ブロック総裁が住宅市場の調整
による景気下振れリスクに言及して慎重姿勢もみせたため、不動産価格の下落が追加利上げ
の制約になるとの見方が広がった。同国の家計債務残高はＧＤＰ比 110％超と主要国のなか
でも突出しており、金利上昇と逆資産効果の双方が消費の重荷になる。 
○ しかし、足元では豪ドルに変化の兆しがある。ハウザー副総裁が、原油高を背景にインフレ
見通しが 「著しく悪化している」 と述べ、 インフレ期待の上昇を防ぐ必要性を強調するなど、
ＲＢＡ高官から相次いでタカ派姿勢を維持する旨の発言が相次いだことが影響している。 
○ ７-９月のインフレ率は前年同期比＋3.9％(前期比＋1.3％)、 コアインフレ率(トリム平均値)
も同＋3.7％(前期比＋1.0％)といずれもＲＢＡの見通しを上回り、基調的な物価圧力の根強
さが示されている。 ９月単月のインフレ率も前年同月比＋4.3％と６ヵ月ぶりの高い伸びとな
り、 エネルギー価格が押し上げた。 一方、 変動の大きい財と観光を除いたベースは同＋3.6％
に鈍化しており、単月の物価はまちまちの様相をみせていると捉えられる。 
○ とはいえ、 ブロック総裁は28 日の議会証言において、 物価上昇圧力が依然強いとの認識を示
し、11 月３日の次回会合で利上げの効果を議論する考えを示した。 市場では追加利上げが意
識されており、当面の豪ドル相場は比較的堅調に推移する可能性が高まっている。

９月以降の金融市場では、豪ドルが弱含む動きをみせてきた。背景には、ＦＲＢ（米連邦準備制
度理事会） が９月に開催したFOMC（連邦公開市場委員会） で利上げを決定し、 その後も追加利上げ
に動くとの観測が強まったことがある。一方、ＲＢＡ（オーストラリア準備銀行）も９月の定例会
合で利上げを再開しており、 年明け以降に計４回利上げを行うなど金融引き締めを強めてきた （注
１）。ＲＢＡは８月定例会合では政策金利を据え置いたものの、追加利上げに言及するなど「タカ

## Document 830: 霍华德·马克斯：一个外行对AI的思考，谁能从AI投资中赚到钱？

Source PDF: all report pdf/霍华德·马克斯：一个外行对AI的思考，谁能从AI投资中赚到钱？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

橡树资本联合创始⼈霍华德 · ⻢克斯发出警告： AI ⾰命⼏乎确定，但投资者能否从中赚钱远
未确定。技术突破不等于投资回报，谁能掌握定价权、谁将沦为基础设施买单者，仍是未
知数。随着万亿美元级资本涌⼊，数据中⼼、债务融资与循环交易⻛险持续累积。⻢克斯
提醒， AI 最⼤的投资陷阱，或许不是技术失灵，⽽是投资者为尚未兑现的利润⽀付了过⾼
代价。
摘要：
橡树资本联合创始⼈霍华德 · ⻢克斯（ Howard Marks ）在最新备忘录中，以⼀个 " 外⾏ " 的视⻆系
统梳理了围绕⼈⼯智能投资的核⼼问题。他的结论并不乐观：AI 投资涉及的关键变量数量异常
庞⼤，预测难度远超以往任何⼀次技术⾰命，⽽真正能从中获利的究竟是谁，⽬前在很⼤程度
上仍是⼀个信仰问题。
⻢克斯在备忘录中援引 Anthropic 旗下 AI 模型 Claude 的评估指出，AI 整体的可预测性评分仅为 3
分（满分 10 分）。其中，技术⽅向的可预测性尚可达到 6 、 7 分，但投资者真正关⼼的问题 —— 盈
利速度、利润规模以及利润归属 —— 可预测性仅约 2 分。这⼀判断直接指向 AI 投资的核⼼困境：

⽅向清晰，但回报⾼度不确定。
⻢克斯同时警告， AI 已成为贯穿整个投资组合的 " 断层线 " 。 AI 相关公司贡献了标普 500 指数⾃
2024 年 1 ⽉以来约三分之⼆的价格涨幅（据摩根⼤通数据），市场对单⼀板块的依赖程度已达到
历史罕⻅⽔平，⼀旦出现负⾯冲击，其影响将远超⼀般⾏业⻛险。
谁能从 AI 中获利：⽤⼾还是⼚商？
⻢克斯在备忘录中提出了⼀个核⼼问题： AI 将提升效率，但效率的提升能否转化为盈利，以及盈
利归谁所有？
他援引巴菲特在互联⽹泡沫时期的判断指出，变⾰性技术未必能回报投资者。如果多个 AI 模型
在某⼀领域的能⼒趋于同质化，其服务将沦为⽆差异化的商品，竞争将压低价格，经济收益可
能更多流向 AI 的使⽤者⽽⾮供应商。在这种情形下，数万亿美元 AI 基础设施投资的回报率将⾯
临严峻考验。
另⼀种可能性则截然相反：部分 AI 公司可能凭借技术壁垒形成垄断地位，其产品对企业的⽣存
与竞争⾄关重要，从⽽获得强⼤的定价权。⻢克斯以英伟达（ Nvidia ）和台积电（ TSMC ）为

例，指出强竞争壁垒带来的丰厚利润已有现实印证。
此外，⻢克斯还提出了⼀个鲜少被讨论的问题： AI 替代劳动⼒后，政府税收将如何弥补？他指
出，如果 AI 取代⼤量⼯⼈，政府可能对 AI 征税，⽽当前许多⾼收⼊者的综合税率已超过 50% ， AI
未来将处于哪个税级，值得投资者认真考量。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

今⽇领先者未必是明⽇赢家
⻢克斯对当前 AI 领域的竞争格局持审慎态度。
他指出，OpenAI 和 Anthropic 在⼏年前对⼤多数⼈⽽⾔还默默⽆闻，这⼀事实本⾝就说明 AI ⾏
业的格局可以在极短时间内发⽣根本性变化。
他以 20 ⾄ 25 年前的互联⽹时代为参照：微软、亚⻢逊、苹果和⾕歌最终胜出，但 Napster 、雅虎
和 Netscape 却销声匿迹。在⼀个快速变化的技术领域维持领先地位殊为不易，⽽ AI 的新颖性和
进步速度更使这⼀挑战倍增。⻢克斯由此追问：今⽇ AI 领导者在五到⼗年后仍保持领先的概率究
竟有多⼤？
他还特别提⽰了地缘政治⻛险。⼏乎所有领先芯⽚均来⾃台积电，美国限制向中国出⼝最先进
芯⽚，这促使中国推出低成本模型以冲击美国市场。⼀旦这⼀格局发⽣变化，美国的竞争优势
将⾯临实质性威胁。
数据中⼼并⾮稳妥的 " 避⻛港 "
对于希望规避技术路线不确定性、转⽽投资基础设施的投资者，⻢克斯提出了 " 卖铲⼦ " 策略的逻
辑 —— ⽆论哪家 AI 公司最终胜出，都需要使⽤基础设施。当前算⼒供不应求，也为数据中⼼投资

增添了吸引⼒。
然⽽，⻢克斯明确指出，数据中⼼并⾮ " 稳赚不赔 " 的选择。他列举了多重⻛险：数据中⼼成本的
⼤头是芯⽚（ GPU ）⽽⾮建筑本⾝，芯⽚的折旧与淘汰⻛险通常由使⽤⽅承担；⼤型选址⾯临
监管障碍和社区阻⼒；电⼒供应、冷却设备和建筑服务均存在潜在瓶颈；部分数据中⼼租约含
有 " 退出条款 " ，允许租⼾在特定条件下终⽌合同；以芯⽚作为抵押品的债务，还⾯临抵押物寿命
短于债务期限的⻛险。
⻢克斯总结认为，数据中⼼投资的成败分化可能⽐⼀般基础设施投资更为悬殊。
信贷市场 ——AI 的资本胃⼝引发连锁⻛险
⻢克斯在备忘录中专⻔审视了 AI 对信贷市场的影响。
据⾼盛数据，今年迄今全球 AI 相关债务发⾏规模已接近 6000 亿美元，其中约 4000 亿美元流⼊美
国投资级债务市场，占美国投资级债券供给总量的逾五分之⼀， AI 相关债券在彭博美元投资级
企业债指数中的占⽐已升⾄约 12% 。
展望未来，⻢克斯估计 AI 每年可能需要平均 1 万亿美元的新增资本，其中相当⼤⽐例将通过债务

融资实现。他对此提出了 " 挤出效应 " 的担忧：对利率不敏感、愿意不惜代价融资的超⼤规模科技
公司，其旺盛的借贷需求可能推⾼整体利率⽔平，包括政府融资成本。
他还对 AI ⾏业内部的 " 循环交易 " 表⽰警惕 —— 芯⽚制造商、超⼤规模科技公司、前沿实验室和数
据中⼼建设⽅之间存在相互融资、相互购买的现象，资⾦有时从 A 流向 B 再回到 A ，有时经由多
⽅辗转循环。⻢克斯质疑，这些交易是否真正基于公平市场原则，还是供应商在补贴买家以维
护市场份额，其中潜藏的相互依赖⻛险不容忽视。
泡沫之问：信仰还是理性？
⾯对市场持续争论的 "AI 是否是泡沫 " 这⼀问题，⻢克斯给出了他的定义：⾮理性繁荣导致的轻率
⾏为、过度投资和过⾼的参与价格。他认为，判断 AI 是否构成泡沫，需要从三个维度分别考量：

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
技术本⾝是否被过度炒作？企业的资本⽀出是否过度且注定回报不⾜？投资者是否承担了过多
⻛险并⽀付了过⾼价格？
⻢克斯表⽰，他仍然相信 AI 技术本⾝将产⽣深远影响，改变企业、经济乃⾄整个社会的⾯
貌， " 技术本⾝不是幻觉 " 。但他坦承，在尚不清楚 AI 将如何被使⽤、谁将从中获利之前，⽆法评
估企业⽀出的合理性，也⽆法判断当前股票价格或债券收益率是否合适。
他援引⼀句被归于美国经济学家 Stuart Chase 的话作为注脚： "对于信者，⽆需证明；对于不信
者，证明亦⽆⽤。 "⻢克斯认为，这句话是⽬前对 AI 投资争议最为贴切的总结。
校准谦逊，⽽⾮回避
尽管充满疑问，⻢克斯并不主张投资者对 AI 袖⼿旁观。他认为， AI 是⾄少⼀代⼈以来最具影响

⼒、同时也最难预测的投资命题，正确的回应不是瘫痪，⽽是 " 校准式谦逊 "—— 在策略选择、仓
位规模和⻛险承担上保持克制，同时愿意在不确定性中采取⾏动。
他提出，不同的 AI 投资形式需要回答不同的问题。投资前沿实验室的股权投资者，需要对技术进
步、竞争格局、定价能⼒、未来盈利和估值同时做出正确判断；⽽为⻓期合同下的数据中⼼提
供贷款的投资者，需要判断的变量更少，但关注点截然不同 —— 租⼾信⽤质量、合同条款、电
⼒供应和资产残值。
⻢克斯建议投资者在做出决策前，扪⼼⾃问以下⼏个问题：需要多少件事同时成真才能获得可
接受的回报？如果核⼼假设被证伪，最⼤损失是多少？哪些⻛险获得了补偿，哪些只是在默默
承担？价格中已经包含了多少乐观预期？这笔投资是放⼤了还是分散了现有组合的⻛险？
他以巴菲特 1979 年刊于《福布斯》的⼀段话作结： "未来从不清晰；在股市中，为皆⼤欢喜的共
识⽀付的代价极为⾼昂。不确定性，实际上是⻓期价值买家的朋友。" ⻢克斯认为，这段写于近
半个世纪前的话，是为这篇 AI 备忘录量⾝定制的完美结语。

## Document 839: 黄仁勋出局，英伟达5000亿美元AI融资计划也悬了

Source PDF: all report pdf/黄仁勋出局，英伟达5000亿美元AI融资计划也悬了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻩仁勋主导的 AI 融资平台⾃ 8 ⽉⾼调宣布⾄今零交易落地，核⼼症结在于英伟达仅承诺 25%
兜底⽐例，远低于竞争对⼿博通（最⾼ 72.5% ），导致贷款⽅兴趣寥寥。分析认为，如今失
去灵魂⼈物，⼜遭华尔街施压要求承担更多⻛险，项⽬落地遥遥⽆期。
摘要：
英伟达创始⼈兼⾸席执⾏官⻩仁勋的突然离职，令这家芯⽚巨头⼀项雄⼼勃勃的 AI 融资计划陷⼊
更深的不确定性。这项由⻩仁勋亲⾃主导、联合华尔街六⼤资产管理巨头打造的 5000 亿美元 AI
计算基础设施融资平台，在⻩仁勋出局之前便已问题重重，如今前景更加扑朔迷离。
据彭博社 11 ⽉ 13 ⽇报道，知情⼈⼠透露，在过去数⽉内未能促成任何交易之后，英伟达近⼀个
⽉来已⾯临来⾃部分客⼾和华尔街合作伙伴的压⼒，要求其提⾼融资兜底⽐例。英伟达⽬前承
诺提供的 “ 剩余价值⽀持协议 ” 上限为交易规模的 25% ，低于竞争对⼿博通（ Broadcom ）的⽔
平，这⼀差距被认为是阻碍交易落地的关键因素之⼀。据其中⼀位知情⼈⼠称，英伟达最早可
能要到明年 1 ⽉才能宣布任何交易。

⻩仁勋的离职使局⾯更加复杂。据华尔街⻅闻⽂章写道，英伟达周四宣布，⾸席财务官 Colette
Kress 将出任临时⾸席执⾏官，⻩仁勋则与公司彻底切割。在⻩仁勋执掌英伟达的三⼗年间，他
是推动这⼀融资平台的核⼼⼈物，其离去令整个项⽬能否延续充满疑问。
华尔街联盟：从⾼光时刻到交易停滞
这⼀融资平台的起点，是今年 8 ⽉⼀幕颇具象征意义的场景。⾝着标志性⽪夹克的⻩仁勋，在电
视镜头前与 Apollo Global Management 、⻉莱德（ BlackRock ）、⿊⽯（ Blackstone ）、
Brookfield Asset Management 、⾼盛（ Goldman Sachs ）和 KKR 的⾼管们并肩亮相，共同宣布
建⽴⼀个旨在为 AI 计算交易提供集体融资的平台，⽬标规模⾼达 5000 亿美元。
然⽽，据彭博报道，在这⼀⾼调宣布的背后，⾼盛、⿊⽯和 Apollo 此前已为相关债务交易运作数
⽉，却未能取得成果。⻩仁勋在公开场合对这⼀ 5000 亿美元计划⼤加赞扬，部分原因在于为其

客⼾群体提振信⼼ —— 这些客⼾正是英伟达未来需求的核⼼⽀撑。这⼀提振来得恰逢其时：就
在宣布前数周，专注 AI 领域的对冲基⾦ Situational Awareness 刚刚遭遇重⼤亏损，冲击波蔓延⾄
科技股和芯⽚股。
如今，距离那次⾼光亮相已过去数⽉，平台尚未促成⼀笔交易落地。
兜底⽐例之争：英伟达与博通的差距
这⼀融资模式的核⼼机制在于 “ 剩余价值⽀持协议 ” 。通常情况下，企业会设⽴特殊⽬的载体来举
债购买芯⽚，再将芯⽚租赁给客⼾使⽤。这类特殊⽬的实体的价值来源于租赁合同产⽣的现⾦
流，以及芯⽚本⾝的残值。
由于贷款⽅担忧持续烧钱的 AI 企业可能违约，芯⽚⼚商提供的兜底⽀持对于吸引贷款⽅⾄关重
要。其运作逻辑是：⼀旦租赁付款中断，相关资产将被重新租出或出售以偿还剩余债务；若仍
存在缺⼝，则由兜底⽅补⾜差额。这⼀机制不仅能吸引更多贷款⽅参与，还有助于获得更⾼信
⽤评级，从⽽降低融资成本。
博通已通过其今年 6 ⽉与 Apollo 和⿊⽯共同建⽴的 “AI XPV” 平台率先落地此类交易。今年 5 ⽉，博

通为⼀笔 350 亿美元债务交易中的 300 亿美元提供了兜底，⽀持 Anthropic 租赁博通与⾕歌旗下
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

图⽚
Alphabet 联合⽣产的张量处理单元（ TPU ）芯⽚，获得兜底⽀持的部分取得了投资级评级和更
低利率。据博通 2026 年 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇提交的 8-K ⽂件，博通还将为另⼀笔 630 亿美元的交易提供
72.5% （约 430 亿美元）的兜底⽀持，同样服务于 Anthropic 。
相⽐之下，英伟达仅承诺提供 25% 的兜底⽐例。据报道，知情⼈⼠表⽰，这⼀差距令英伟达的
交易安排更难推进，尤其是在市场上同时存在条款更为优厚的竞争性交易的情况下。
对英伟达⽽⾔，提⾼兜底⽐例并⾮没有代价。若英伟达坚持 25% 的上限，其芯⽚对 AI 和云计算
客⼾⽽⾔将更难负担，对贷款⽅的吸引⼒也会下降；但若提⾼兜底⽐例，则意味着承担更多⻛
险，同时也与其试图借助第三⽅资⾦推动 AI 基础设施扩张的初衷相悖。

报道称，这⼀平台的设计逻辑，正是希望通过引⼊外部资本来撬动 AI 算⼒投资的下⼀轮增⻓，⽽
⾮由英伟达⾃⾝承担过多资产负债表⻛险。
⻩仁勋离去：平台失去核⼼推⼿
在⻩仁勋执掌英伟达的三⼗年间，他是这家公司唯⼀的⾸席执⾏官。在他的主导下，英伟达被
称为 “AI 的中央银⾏ ” ，通过在整个 AI ⽣态系统中持股并提供兜底⽀持，为这⼀仍处于早期阶段的
⾏业建⽴信⼼。
这⼀⻆⾊在 8 ⽉的融资平台宣布中得到了最集中的体现。然⽽，随着⻩仁勋因婚外情丑闻⽽被迫
出局，这⼀平台的推进失去了最核⼼的驱动⼒。
Colette Kress 接任临时⾸席执⾏官，但英伟达⾯临的问题已不仅仅是⼈事更迭。据报道，知情
⼈⼠表⽰，⻩仁勋的离职是否会令相关交易脱轨，乃⾄令整个项⽬陷⼊停滞，⽬前仍是未知
数。可以确定的是，下⼀次提振市场信⼼的⼒量，将不得不来⾃⻩仁勋以外的地⽅。
